of beads written to JSONL.\n    Flushed(usize),\n    /// Flush was attempted but failed; carries the human-readable warning.\n    Failed(String),\n}\n\nimpl FlushOutcome {\n    /// The warning string to surface (JSON `warning` key / stderr), if any.\n    ///\n    /// `Disabled` and `Flushed` are silent; only `Failed` yields `Some`.\n    pub fn warning(&self) -> Option<&str> {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Failed(msg) => Some(msg.as_str()),\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Flushed(_) => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Number of beads flushed, or `0` when disabled/failed.\n    pub fn flushed_count(&self) -> usize {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Flushed(n) => *n,\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Failed(_) => 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the flush was attempted and failed.\n    pub fn is_failure(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, FlushOutcome::Failed(_))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Perform the dirty-only flush of `workspace_dir`'s beads to JSONL.\n///\n/// Thin wrapper over [`crate::sync::flush_dirty`]; returns the number of beads\n/// written (`0` when nothing was dirty).\npub fn run(workspace_dir: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<usize> {\n    flush_dirty(workspace_dir)\n}\n\n/// Run auto-flush after a mutation, honoring a pre-resolved `enabled` decision,\n/// and return an outcome whose [`FlushOutcome::warning`] bridges into the\n/// JSON/stderr warning channel.\n///\n/// A failed flush is intentionally non-fatal: the mutation already succeeded,\n/// so the caller degrades to a warning rather than an error.\npub fn after_mutation(workspace_dir: &Path, enabled: bool) -> FlushOutcome {\n    if !enabled {\n        return FlushOutcome::Disabled;\n    }\n    match run(workspace_dir) {\n        Ok(count) => FlushOutcome::Flushed(count),\n        Err(e) => FlushOutcome::Failed(format!(\"auto-flush to JSONL failed: {e}\")),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convenience wrapper that resolves enablement from `config` + the CLI flag\n/// via [`enabled`] before delegating to [`after_mutation`].\npub fn after_mutation_with_config(\n    workspace_dir: &Path,\n    config: &Config,\n    no_auto_flush_flag: bool,\n) -> FlushOutcome {\n    after_mutation(workspace_dir, enabled(config, no_auto_flush_flag))\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::config::{init_workspace, Config};\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n    use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n    fn config_with_auto_flush(value: bool) -> Config {\n        let mut cfg = Config::default();\n        cfg.sync.auto_flush = value;\n        cfg\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn enabled_true_only_when_config_on_and_flag_off() {\n        assert!(enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(true), false));\n        // CLI override wins over an enabled config.\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(true), true));\n        // Config off disables regardless of the flag.\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(false), false));\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(false), true));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn disabled_outcome_when_not_enabled() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let outcome = after_mutation(tmp.path(), false);\n        assert!(matches!(outcome, FlushOutcome::Disabled));\n        assert_eq!(outcome.warning(), None, \"disabled flush must be silent\");\n        assert!(!outcome.is_failure());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn success_yields_no_warning() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let beads_dir = tmp.path().join(\".beads\");\n        init_workspace(&beads_dir, \"bf\").unwrap();\n        let storage = Storage::open(&beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\")).unwrap();\n\n        // A mutation marks the bead dirty, so the flush has something to write.\n        let issue = crate::model::Issue::new(\"bf-1\".into(), \"T\".into(), \".\".into());\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n        storage.mark_dirty(\"bf-1\").unwrap();\n\n        let outcome = after_mutation(tmp.path(), true);\n        assert!(\n            matches!(outcome, FlushOutcome::Flushed(1)),\n            \"expected Flushed(1), got {outcome:?}\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(outcome.warning(), None, \"successful flush must be silent\");\n        assert!(tmp.path().join(\".beads/issues.jsonl\").exists());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn forced_failure_yields_populated_warning() {\n        // Real workspace with a dirty bead, but the JSONL export target is a\n        // directory — the atomic temp+rename can't overwrite it, so the flush\n        // fails and the outcome carries a warning.\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let beads_dir = tmp.path().join(\".beads\");\n        init_workspace(&beads_dir, \"bf\").unwrap();\n        let storage = Storage::open(&beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\")).unwrap();\n        let issue = crate::model::Issue::new(\"bf-1\".into(), \"T\".into(), \".\".into());\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n        storage.mark_dirty(\"bf-1\").unwrap();\n        // Wedge the flush: make issues.jsonl an (undeletable-by-rename) directory.\n        std::fs::create_dir(beads_dir.join(\"issues.jsonl\")).unwrap();\n\n        let outcome = after_mutation(tmp.path(), true);\n        assert!(outcome.is_failure(), \"expected failure, got {outcome:?}\");\n        let warning = outcome.warning().expect(\"failure must populate a warning\");\n        assert!(\n            warning.contains(\"auto-flush to JSONL failed\"),\n            \"warning must describe the auto-flush failure, got: {warning}\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn with_config_resolves_before_running() {\n        // Config disables → treated as Disabled even though the flag is off and\n        // a real workspace exists.\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let beads_dir = tmp.path().join(\".beads\");\n        init_workspace(&beads_dir, \"bf\").unwrap();\n        let _ = Storage::open(&beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\")).unwrap();\n\n        let outcome = after_mutation_with_config(tmp.path(), &config_with_auto_flush(false), false);\n        assert!(matches!(outcome, FlushOutcome::Disabled));\n        assert_eq!(outcome.warning(), None);\n Integration test for the `bf comments add` / `comments list` CLI round-trip.\n//!\n//! Ports the scenario exercised by the orphaned root-level script\n//! `test_bf_test3.sh` (removed in the bf-3o9 repo-hygiene cleanup) into a real\n//! cargo integration test. The storage layer (`add_comment` / `list_comments`)\n//! is already exercised by `dirty_tracking.rs` and the `comments` table schema\n//! is checked by `br_isolation.rs`, but no committed test drove the\n//! `comments add` -> `comments list` CLI path end-to-end until now (audit\n//! bf-5wz0l flagged this as one of two coverage gaps).\n\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::process::Command;\nuse tempfile::TempDir;\n\nfn bf() -> Command {\n    Command::new(env!(\"CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf\"))\n}\n\n/// Run `bf` with args in `workspace`, returning (stdout, stderr, success).\nfn run_bf(workspace: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> (String, String, bool) {\n    let output = bf()\n        .current_dir(workspace)\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .expect(\"failed to execute bf\");\n    (\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string(),\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string(),\n        output.status.success(),\n    )\n}\n\nfn setup() -> (TempDir, PathBuf) {\n    let temp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n    let workspace = temp.path().to_path_buf();\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&workspace, &[\"init\", \"--prefix\", \"bf\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"bf init failed: {e}\");\n    (temp, workspace)\n}\n\n/// Create a bead and return its bare ID (create prints the bare ID on stdout).\nfn create_bead(workspace: &Path, title: &str) -> String {\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(workspace, &[\"create\", \"--title\", title]);\n    assert!(ok, \"bf create failed: {err}\");\n    let id = out.trim().to_string();\n    assert!(!id.is_empty(), \"create produced no id: {out}\");\n    id\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn comments_add_and_list_round_trip() {\n    let (_temp, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"Test bead for comments round-trip\");\n\n    // A bead with no comments reports so.\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"list\", &id]);\n    assert!(ok, \"comments list failed: {err}\");\n    assert!(out.contains(\"No comments\"), \"unexpected empty-list output: {out}\");\n\n    // Add a single comment.\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"add\", &id, \"This is a test comment\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"comments add failed: {err}\");\n    assert!(out.contains(\"Added comment\"), \"add did not confirm: {out}\");\n\n    // Listing now surfaces the comment body.\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"list\", &id]);\n    assert!(ok, \"comments list failed: {err}\");\n    assert!(\n        out.contains(\"This is a test comment\"),\n        \"comment body missing from list: {out}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn comments_list_preserves_insertion_order() {\n    let (_temp, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"Multi-comment bead\");\n\n    let bodies = [\"First comment\", \"Second comment\", \"Third comment\"];\n    for body in &bodies {\n        let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"add\", &id, body]);\n        assert!(ok, \"comments add failed: {e}\");\n    }\n\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"list\", &id]);\n    assert!(ok, \"comments list failed: {err}\");\n\n    // All three present, in the order they were added.\n    let first = out\n        .find(\"First comment\")\n        .expect(\"First comment missing\");\n    let second = out\n        .find(\"Second comment\")\n        .expect(\"Second comment missing\");\n    let third = out\n        .find(\"Third comment\")\n        .expect(\"Third comment missing\");\n    assert!(first < second && second < third, \"order not preserved: {out}\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn comments_add_joins_multiple_text_args() {\n    // The CLI joins multiple text args with spaces, so quoting is optional.\n    let (_temp, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"Join-args bead\");\n\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"add\", &id, \"multi\", \"word\", \"comment\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"comments add failed: {err}\");\n    assert!(out.contains(\"Added comment\"), \"add did not confirm: {out}\");\n\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"list\", &id]);\n    assert!(ok, \"comments list failed: {err}\");\n    assert!(\n        out.contains(\"multi word comment\"),\n        \"joined text not round-tripped: {out}\"\n {"parentUuid":"245a6f34-0f0d-4eed-8bcb-83fd773f7dd2","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdHHhfLKENcLHY2WtfYtf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CYGPGq6sCaPpBKW1AARgRs","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/coding/bead-forge/tests/autoflush_mutation.rs","content":"//! End-to-end tests for auto-flush wiring on single-issue mutations\n//! (Phase 7.1 child 2/5, bf-3iosi).\n//!\n//! Every single-issue mutation handler (`create`, `update`, `close`, `reopen`,\n//! `dep add/remove`, `label add/remove`, `comments add`, `annotate set/…`) runs\n//! a best-effort SQLite→JSONL flush after its storage write commits, honoring\n//! the effective auto-flush switch (`config.sync.auto_flush && !--no-auto-flush`)\n//! and surfacing a flush failure through the warning channel WITHOUT failing the\n//! mutation. These tests drive the real `bf` binary against a tempdir workspace\n//! and assert on `issues.jsonl` (the flush target), matching the CLI-spawn shape\n//! already used by `comments_cli.rs`.\n\nuse serde_json::Value;\nuse std::fs;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::process::Command;\nuse tempfile::TempDir;\n\nfn bf() -> Command {\n    Command::new(env!(\"CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf\"))\n}\n\n/// Run `bf` with args in `workspace`, returning (stdout, stderr, success).\nfn run_bf(workspace: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> (String, String, bool) {\n    let output = bf()\n        .current_dir(workspace)\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .expect(\"failed to execute bf\");\n    (\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string(),\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string(),\n        output.status.success(),\n    )\n}\n\nfn setup() -> (TempDir, PathBuf) {\n    let temp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n    let workspace = temp.path().to_path_buf();\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&workspace, &[\"init\", \"--prefix\", \"bf\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"bf init failed: {e}\");\n    (temp, workspace)\n}\n\n/// Create a bead and return its bare ID (create prints the bare ID on stdout).\nfn create_bead(workspace: &Path, title: &str) -> String {\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(workspace, &[\"create\", \"--title\", title]);\n    assert!(ok, \"bf create failed: {err}\");\n    let id = out.trim().to_string();\n    assert!(!id.is_empty(), \"create produced no id: {out}\");\n    id\n}\n\nfn jsonl_path(workspace: &Path) -> PathBuf {\n    workspace.join(\".beads\").join(\"issues.jsonl\")\n}\n\n/// Parse every non-empty line of `issues.jsonl` into a JSON object.\nfn read_beads(workspace: &Path) -> Vec<Value> {\n    let path = jsonl_path(workspace);\n    let content = fs::read_to_string(&path)\n        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!(\"issues.jsonl unreadable at {}: {e}\", path.display()));\n    content\n        .lines()\n        .filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty())\n        .map(|l| serde_json::from_str::<Value>(l).expect(\"issues.jsonl line is not valid JSON\"))\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// Locate a bead by id in `issues.jsonl`, failing loudly if the flush did not\n/// write it. This is the core assertion shared by every \"auto-flush on\" test:\n/// the changed bead must be present immediately after the mutation returns.\nfn find_bead(workspace: &Path, id: &str) -> Value {\n    read_beads(workspace)\n        .into_iter()\n        .find(|b| b.get(\"id\").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) == Some(id))\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {id} not found in issues.jsonl after mutation\"))\n}\n\nfn field<'a>(bead: &'a Value, key: &str) -> &'a str {\n    bead.get(key)\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead missing string field '{key}': {bead}\"))\n}\n\n// --- Auto-flush ON (the default): each mutation lands in issues.jsonl. ---\n\n#[test]\nfn create_flushes_new_bead() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"Created bead\");\n    let bead = find_bead(&ws, &id);\n    assert_eq!(field(&bead, \"title\"), \"Created bead\");\n    assert_eq!(field(&bead, \"status\"), \"open\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn update_flushes_changed_status() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To update\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"update\", &id, \"--status\", \"in_progress\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"update failed: {e}\");\n    assert_eq!(field(&find_bead(&ws, &id), \"status\"), \"in_progress\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn close_flushes_closed_status_and_reason() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To close\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"close\", &id, \"--reason\", \"all done\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"close failed: {e}\");\n    let bead = find_bead(&ws, &id);\n    assert_eq!(field(&bead, \"status\"), \"closed\");\n    assert_eq!(field(&bead, \"close_reason\"), \"all done\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn reopen_flushes_reopened_status() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To reopen\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"close\", &id, \"--reason\", \"done\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"close failed: {e}\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"reopen\", &id]);\n    assert!(ok, \"reopen failed: {e}\");\n    assert_eq!(field(&find_bead(&ws, &id), \"status\"), \"open\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn dep_add_flushes_dependency() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let blocker = create_bead(&ws, \"Blocker\");\n    let blocked = create_bead(&ws, \"Blocked\");\n    // `dep add <blocker> --blocks <blocked>`: the blocked bead gains a dependency\n    // on the blocker, so its flushed line carries the depends_on edge.\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"dep\", \"add\", &blocker, \"--blocks\", &blocked]);\n    assert!(ok, \"dep add failed: {e}\");\n    let bead = find_bead(&ws, &blocked);\n    let deps = bead\n        .get(\"dependencies\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_array())\n        .expect(\"dependencies array missing\");\n    assert!(\n        deps.iter()\n            .any(|d| d.get(\"depends_on_id\").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) == Some(blocker.as_str())),\n        \"blocked bead does not depend on blocker in issues.jsonl: {bead}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn label_add_flushes_label() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To label\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"label\", \"add\", &id, \"--label\", \"urgent\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"label add failed: {e}\");\n    let bead = find_bead(&ws, &id);\n    let labels = bead\n        .get(\"labels\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_array())\n        .expect(\"labels array missing\");\n    assert!(\n        labels.iter().any(|l| l.as_str() == Some(\"urgent\")),\n        \"label 'urgent' not flushed to issues.jsonl: {bead}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn comment_add_flushes_comment() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To comment\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"comments\", \"add\", &id, \"a\", \"flushed\", \"comment\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"comments add failed: {e}\");\n    let bead = find_bead(&ws, &id);\n    let comments = bead\n        .get(\"comments\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_array())\n        .expect(\"comments array missing\");\n    assert!(\n        comments.iter().any(|c| c\n            .get(\"text\")\n            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n            .map(|t| t.contains(\"a flushed comment\"))\n            .unwrap_or(false)),\n        \"comment body not flushed to issues.jsonl: {bead}\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn annotate_set_triggers_flush() {\n    // Annotations are not serialized as a top-level JSONL field, so we assert\n    // the flush *happened* rather than its content: create clears the dirty\n    // marks and writes issues.jsonl, we delete it, and `annotate set` must\n    // re-flush the (now-dirty-again) bead, recreating the file.\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To annotate\");\n    fs::remove_file(jsonl_path(&ws)).unwrap();\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"annotate\", \"set\", &id, \"env\", \"prod\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"annotate set failed: {e}\");\n    assert!(\n        jsonl_path(&ws).exists(),\n        \"annotate set did not re-flush issues.jsonl\"\n    );\n    // The re-flushed file must contain the annotated bead.\n    find_bead(&ws, &id);\n}\n\n// --- Auto-flush OFF: the switch suppresses the flush. ---\n\n#[test]\nfn no_auto_flush_flag_leaves_jsonl_untouched() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    // Create with the flag: no flush, so no issues.jsonl is produced.\n    let (out, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"--no-auto-flush\", \"create\", \"--title\", \"No flush\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"create failed: {e}\");\n    let id = out.trim().to_string();\n    assert!(\n        !jsonl_path(&ws).exists(),\n        \"--no-auto-flush create must not write issues.jsonl\"\n    );\n    // A subsequent flagged mutation must likewise leave it absent.\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"--no-auto-flush\", \"update\", &id, \"--status\", \"in_progress\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"update failed: {e}\");\n    assert!(\n        !jsonl_path(&ws).exists(),\n        \"--no-auto-flush update must not write issues.jsonl\"\n    );\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn config_auto_flush_false_leaves_jsonl_untouched() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    // Persist the config master switch off; no CLI flag needed.\n    let cfg = ws.join(\".beads\").join(\"config.yaml\");\n    let mut content = fs::read_to_string(&cfg).unwrap();\n    content.push_str(\"sync:\\n  auto_flush: false\\n\");\n    fs::write(&cfg, content).unwrap();\n\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"create\", \"--title\", \"Config off\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"create failed: {e}\");\n    assert!(\n        !jsonl_path(&ws).exists(),\n        \"sync.auto_flush:false must not write issues.jsonl\"\n    );\n}\n\n// --- Flush failure is non-fatal and surfaces a warning. ---\n\n/// Wedge the flush by making `issues.jsonl` a directory: the atomic temp+rename\n/// export cannot overwrite it, so the flush fails while the mutation itself\n/// already committed.\nfn wedge_flush(ws: &Path) {\n    let path = jsonl_path(ws);\n    if path.exists() {\n        fs::remove_file(&path).ok();\n    }\n    fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn flush_failure_nonfatal_json_warning_and_dirty_retained() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    wedge_flush(&ws);\n\n    // Mutation succeeds (exit 0) despite the wedged flush; --json carries a\n    // top-level \"warning\" alongside the \"id\".\n    let (out, _e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"create\", \"--json\", \"--title\", \"Wedged\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"create must not fail on a flush error\");\n    let parsed: Value = serde_json::from_str(out.trim()).expect(\"create --json emitted invalid JSON\");\n    let id = parsed\n        .get(\"id\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .expect(\"create --json missing id\")\n        .to_string();\n    assert!(\n        parsed\n            .get(\"warning\")\n            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n            .map(|w| w.contains(\"auto-flush\"))\n            .unwrap_or(false),\n        \"create --json must carry an auto-flush warning: {parsed}\"\n    );\n\n    // The dirty mark is retained: once the wedge is cleared, an explicit\n    // flush-only sync End-to-end tests for auto-flush wiring on single-issue mutations\n//! (Phase 7.1 child 2/5, bf-3iosi).\n//!\n//! Every single-issue mutation handler (`create`, `update`, `close`, `reopen`,\n//! `dep add/remove`, `label add/remove`, `comments add`, `annotate set/…`) runs\n//! a best-effort SQLite→JSONL flush after its storage write commits, honoring\n//! the effective auto-flush switch (`config.sync.auto_flush && !--no-auto-flush`)\n//! and surfacing a flush failure through the warning channel WITHOUT failing the\n//! mutation. These tests drive the real `bf` binary against a tempdir workspace\n//! and assert on `issues.jsonl` (the flush target), matching the CLI-spawn shape\n//! already used by `comments_cli.rs`.\n\nuse serde_json::Value;\nuse std::fs;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::process::Command;\nuse tempfile::TempDir;\n\nfn bf() -> Command {\n    Command::new(env!(\"CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf\"))\n}\n\n/// Run `bf` with args in `workspace`, returning (stdout, stderr, success).\nfn run_bf(workspace: &Path, args: &[&str]) -> (String, String, bool) {\n    let output = bf()\n        .current_dir(workspace)\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .expect(\"failed to execute bf\");\n    (\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string(),\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string(),\n        output.status.success(),\n    )\n}\n\nfn setup() -> (TempDir, PathBuf) {\n    let temp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n    let workspace = temp.path().to_path_buf();\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&workspace, &[\"init\", \"--prefix\", \"bf\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"bf init failed: {e}\");\n    (temp, workspace)\n}\n\n/// Create a bead and return its bare ID (create prints the bare ID on stdout).\nfn create_bead(workspace: &Path, title: &str) -> String {\n    let (out, err, ok) = run_bf(workspace, &[\"create\", \"--title\", title]);\n    assert!(ok, \"bf create failed: {err}\");\n    let id = out.trim().to_string();\n    assert!(!id.is_empty(), \"create produced no id: {out}\");\n    id\n}\n\nfn jsonl_path(workspace: &Path) -> PathBuf {\n    workspace.join(\".beads\").join(\"issues.jsonl\")\n}\n\n/// Parse every non-empty line of `issues.jsonl` into a JSON object.\nfn read_beads(workspace: &Path) -> Vec<Value> {\n    let path = jsonl_path(workspace);\n    let content = fs::read_to_string(&path)\n        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!(\"issues.jsonl unreadable at {}: {e}\", path.display()));\n    content\n        .lines()\n        .filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty())\n        .map(|l| serde_json::from_str::<Value>(l).expect(\"issues.jsonl line is not valid JSON\"))\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// Locate a bead by This is the core assertion shared by every \"auto-flush on\" test:\n/// the changed bead must be present immediately after the mutation returns.\nfn find_bead(workspace: &Path, id: &str) -> Value {\n mutation\"))\n}\n\nfn field<'a>(bead: &'a Value, key: &str) -> &'a str {\n    bead.get(key)\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead missing string field '{key}': {bead}\"))\n}\n\n// --- Auto-flush ON (the default): each mutation lands in issues.jsonl. ---\n\n#[test]\nfn create_flushes_new_bead() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"Created bead\");\n    let bead = find_bead(&ws, &id);\n    assert_eq!(field(&bead, \"title\"), \"Created bead\");\n    assert_eq!(field(&bead, \"status\"), \"open\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn update_flushes_changed_status() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To update\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"update\", &id, \"--status\", \"in_progress\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"update failed: {e}\");\n    assert_eq!(field(&find_bead(&ws, &id), \"status\"), \"in_progress\");\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn close_flushes_closed_status_and_reason() {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let id = create_bead(&ws, \"To close\");\n    let (_o, e, ok) = run_bf(&ws, &[\"close\", &id, \"--reason\", \"all done\"]);\n    assert!(ok, \"close failed: {e}\");\n    let bead = {\n    let (_t, ws) = setup();\n    let blocker = create_bead(&ws, \"Blocker\");\n    let blocked = create_bead(&ws, \"Blocked\");\n    // `dep add <blocker> --blocks <blocked>`: the blocked bead The shared index held THEIR staged changes.\n14\t\n15\t**How to apply:**\n16\t- Before committing, run `git status` — there may be staged changes you did NOT make.\n17\t- NEVER use bare `git commit -m` or `git commit -a` / `git add -A`: that sweeps a sibling's staged WIP into your commit.\n18\t- Commit a single path explicitly: `git commit <your-file> -m \"...\"`. The pathspec form commits only that file and leaves the sibling's other staged entries in the index.\n19\t- **Pathspec ordering matters:** put `-m \"msg\"` BEFORE the `-- <file>` separator. `git commit -- <file> -m \"msg\"` fails (`-m` parsed as a pathspec → \"pathspec '-m' did not match\"). Correct: `git commit -m \"msg\" -- notes/bf-XXX.md`.\n20\t- **Recovery if you already committed sibling WIP** (confirmed 2026-07-22, bead bf-5wz0l): `git reset --soft HEAD~1` undoes your commit while keeping the index intact (sibling's staged files stay staged, exactly as before), then re-commit your single path with the pathspec form. Only safe if you haven't pushed yet — a pushed bad commit would need a revert, not a reset (never force-push).\n21\t- Uncommitted sibling WIP may be broken (it broke `test_jsonl` parity mid-flight). Don't \"fix\" or inherit it — leave it for its owner.\n22\t- **When your single-path commit's `git push` is rejected (non-fast-forward)** because a sibling pushed to the shared branch while you worked: do NOT `git pull --rebase` / `--autostash` in the shared tree. A sibling's staged WIP often touches the *same* file the remote's new commit changed (e.g. both editing `src/cli/mod.rs`), so the autostash pop merges → conflict markers land in their in-progress file, corrupting it. Instead, push from an **isolated worktree** so the shared tree is never touched, stashed, or rebased (confirmed 2026-07-22, bead bf-37bzi):\n23\t  ```bash\n24\t  git fetch origin <branch>\n25\t  git worktree add /tmp/push origin/<branch>          # clean, at remote tip\n26\t  git -C /tmp/push cherry-pick <your-local-commit>    # disjoint file → clean pick\n27\t  git -C /tmp/push push origin HEAD:<branch>          # fast-forward over the new tip\n28\t  git worktree remove /tmp/push --force\n29\t  ```\n30\t  Your commit (a disjoint notes/doc file) cherry-picks cleanly; the push is a pure fast-forward; the sibling's staged `MM` work in the main tree stays byte-for-byte intact. The local branch is left \"diverged\" (your commit on the old base vs the remote's cherry-picked twin) — that's fine; the owner's next rebase skips the now-duplicate commit automatically. Use this whenever the shared tree holds another agent's conflicting uncommitted work.\n31\t- `br` is a shim that execs `bf`; no original beads_rust source remains on this box, so \"match br exactly\" is validated by the `tests/test_jsonl.rs` parity test, not by diffing against br.\n32\t"}]},"uuid":"7f9bec04-1979-44b5-a4bf-21d310556db7","timestamp":"2026-07-22T15:00:26.827Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/coding/.claude/projects/-home-coding-bead-forge/memory/bead-forge-shared-workspace-races.md","content":"---\nname: bead-forge-shared-workspace-races\ndescription: bead-forge runs under needle with many concurrent agents sharing ONE working tree — staged/index races are real\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: project\n  originSessionId: 25694869-0403-4818-b646-f9714e4d3389\n  modified: 2026-07-22T14:55:34.077Z\n---\n\nThe `/home/coding/bead-forge` repo is driven by **needle** (`~/.local/bin/needle supervise --workspace /home/coding`), which fans out many concurrent agent identifiers (victor, quebec, papa, sierra, alpha, …) that all operate on the **same git working tree + index**, not isolated worktrees.\n\nObserved on 2026-07-22 (bead bf-doiq, agent victor): while I worked, a sibling agent staged `src/format/json.rs`, `tests/ready_json_fields.rs`, `tests/test_jsonl.rs`, `notes/bf-64zt.md` and wrote `json.rs` ~5s before I checked. The shared index held THEIR staged changes.\n\n**How to apply:**\n- Before committing, run `git status` — there may be staged changes you did NOT make.\n- NEVER use bare `git commit -m` or `git commit -a` / `git add -A`: that sweeps a sibling's staged WIP into your commit.\n- Commit a single path explicitly: `git commit <your-file> -m \"...\"`. The pathspec form commits only that file and leaves the sibling's other staged entries in the index.\n- **Pathspec ordering matters:** put `-m \"msg\"` BEFORE the `-- <file>` separator. `git commit -- <file> -m \"msg\"` fails (`-m` parsed as a pathspec → \"pathspec '-m' did not match\"). Correct: `git commit -m \"msg\" -- notes/bf-XXX.md`.\n- **Recovery if you already committed sibling WIP** (confirmed 2026-07-22, bead bf-5wz0l): `git reset --soft HEAD~1` undoes your commit while keeping the index intact (sibling's staged files stay staged, exactly as before), then re-commit your single path with the pathspec form. Only safe if you haven't pushed yet — a pushed bad commit would need a revert, not a reset (never force-push).\n- Uncommitted sibling WIP may be broken (it broke `test_jsonl` parity mid-flight). Don't \"fix\" or inherit it — leave it for its owner.\n- **When your single-path commit's `git push` is rejected (non-fast-forward)** because a sibling pushed to the shared branch while you worked: do NOT `git pull --rebase` / `--autostash` in the shared tree. A sibling's staged WIP often touches the *same* file the remote's new commit changed (e.g. both editing `src/cli/mod.rs`), so the autostash pop merges → conflict markers land in their in-progress file, corrupting it. Instead, push from an **isolated worktree** so the shared tree is never touched, stashed, or rebased (confirmed 2026-07-22, bead bf-37bzi):\n  ```bash\n  git fetch origin <branch>\n  git worktree add /tmp/push origin/<branch>          # clean, at remote tip\n  git -C /tmp/push cherry-pick <your-local-commit>    # disjoint file → clean pick\n  git -C /tmp/push push origin HEAD:<branch>          # fast-forward over the new tip\n  git worktree remove /tmp/push --force\n  ```\n  Your commit (a disjoint notes/doc file) cherry-picks cleanly; the push is a pure fast-forward; the sibling's staged `MM` work in the main tree stays byte-for-byte intact. The local branch is left \"diverged\" (your commit on the old base vs the remote's cherry-picked twin) — that's fine; the owner's next rebase skips the now-duplicate commit automatically.
